## `AFG-4742-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01`

- **Country:** `AFG` · **Track:** `ENG`
- **Role:** Audio-THD-Analyzer Metrologist
- **Scenario:** Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Audio THD+N Distortion & Video Projector ANSI Lumens Calibrator
- **Intro:** Audio-THD-Analyzer Metrologist — Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Audio THD+N Distortion & Video Projector ANSI Lumens Calibrator — Pre-Calibration Safety and Equipment Verification: Ambient temperature: 23°C ± 2°C; Humidity: 45% ± 10% RH; Calibration certificate validity: ≤ 12 months.
- **Key tolerance:** Ambient temperature: 23°C ± 2°C; Humidity: 45% ± 10% RH; Calibration certificate validity: ≤ 12 months.
- **Standards:** `ANSI/ASA S1.4-2014 (Electroacoustics - Sound Calibrators)`, `IEC 60268-3:2018`, `IEC 61947-1:2002`, `ISO 10012:2003`
